# Psalms (Hebrew, WLC)
Source: Open Scriptures morphhb, Westminster Leningrad Codex 4.20, consonants only, standard values (gadol in TSV)
Verses: 2527  Words: 19587  Letters: 78833  Standard total: 4937962
Format per verse: heading, native line, number line (standard values, word groups separated by ·).
The machine file 27-Ps.tsv carries per-verse letter strings, standard and ordinal streams and the gadol stream.
